TL;DR: 本文尝试构建一个统摄学习、优化与建模的统一理论框架，将学习视为多层级互联过程，通过定义“可解问题”和“参数化方法”，提出一个“通用收敛定理”，将学习研究简化为动力系统、几何和基础物理中的少数核心思想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性模型与优化方法在众多领域取得成功，但缺乏统一的理论框架来引导学习、优化和建模领域的讨论与发现。作者希望借鉴物理理论中从简洁基础涌现丰富现象的类似关系，构建一个稀疏统一的理论体系。

Method: 将学习视为问题设置、方法选择及其通过优化动力学互动的多层级互联过程；首先提出“可解问题”的精确定义，然后定义用于学习其解的“参数化方法”，最终勾勒一个“通用收敛定理”以说明可解问题何时及如何适用于所选方法。

Result: 发现这些建构将学习研究简化为极少数核心思想与工具，其中许多可直接从动力系统理论、几何学和基础物理中借鉴。

Conclusion: 存在一个可能的学习统一理论框架，能够将不同领域的学习问题归约到共同的思想基础，从而指导更系统的讨论与发现。

Abstract: Nonlinear models and optimization methods have successfully tackled a rapidly growing set of problems in recent years. Indeed, a relatively small toolbox of such models and methods can provide sufficient performance across a large landscape of tasks: deep learning alone has made significant recent contributions in scientific modelling, natural language processing, visual analysis, etc. A similar relationship exists between physical theories and phenomena, where many applications and observations emerge neatly from remarkably minimal foundations. It is natural to wonder if sparse unified frameworks could be built to steer discussion and discovery in the fields concerned with learning, optimization, and modelling. In this work, we posit and examine a possible outline for such a unified theory, interpreting the notion of ''learning'' in a broad sense. In particular, we pursue our goals by viewing learning as an inter-connected process on multiple levels: problem setup, choosing methods, and the analysis of their interplay via imposed optimisation dynamics. We begin by proposing a precise yet versatile definition for ''solvable'' problems. We then define the ''parametrised methods'' by which their solution(s) may be ''learned''. Our goal is to sketch a ''universal convergence theorem'', specifying how and when solvable problems become amenable to the methods chosen for them. We find these constructions reduce the study of learning down to remarkably few ideas and tools - many of which are simply adapted from existing ones in dynamical systems theory, geometry, and fundamental physics.

</details>


### [106] [Nonsmooth Riemannian optimization with inexact manifold primitives via bundle methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27078)
*Mateo Díaz,Benjamin Grimmer,Ian McPherson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Hadamard流形上的非光滑测地凸优化问题，提出了近端束方法，并建立了首个仅依赖次梯度和非精确原始算子（一阶收缩和向量传输）的Oracle复杂度界，获得了通用目标下的次线性收敛率和尖峰函数增长下的最优线性收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Hadamard流形优化方法要么需要精确的指数映射和平行传输（计算昂贵），要么缺乏非渐近收敛率保证，亟需一种既实用（允许近似原始算子）又有理论保证（非渐近Oracle复杂度）的算法。

Method: 提出近端束方法（Proximal Bundle Method），仅使用次梯度和一阶近似的收缩与向量传输（非精确原始算子），而非昂贵的指数映射和平行传输。

Result: 建立了首个依赖非精确原始算子的Oracle复杂度界：对一般目标函数获得次线性收敛率，对满足尖峰函数增长（sharp function growth）的目标获得最优线性收敛率。

Conclusion: 表明在Hadamard流形上，使用计算成本更低的近似几何原始算子（一阶收缩和向量传输）即可实现与非精确欧氏方法相匹配的收敛速度，填补了理论空白。

Abstract: Optimization on Hadamard manifolds -- the natural Riemannian setting for globally geodesically convex problems -- relies on exponential maps to retract tangent vectors and parallel transport to connect tangent spaces across the manifold. These primitives are often computationally expensive, leading software packages to rely on approximations: first-order retractions and vector transports. However, existing results for optimization on Hadamard manifolds either require exact primitives or lack non-asymptotic rates. We bridge this gap by introducing a proximal bundle method for nonsmooth geodesically convex optimization and establishing the first oracle-complexity bounds that rely only on subgradients and inexact primitives. We obtain sublinear rates for general objectives and optimal linear convergence under sharp function growth.

</details>


### [107] [A Scale-Shape Dual Newton Method for Entropic Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27154)
*Nicholas Barnfield,James V. Burke,Michael P. Friedlander,Tim Hoheisel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种带阻尼的非精确牛顿法，用于求解非负象限上的熵正则化最小二乘问题，具有全局线性收敛（O(log ε⁻¹)迭代复杂度）、局部超线性到二次收敛速度，且能避免有限精度溢出问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典对偶求解器在处理熵正则化最小二乘时存在有限精度溢出问题，且现有方法缺乏全局收敛保证和理论上的迭代复杂度分析。本文旨在设计一种兼具全局收敛性、快速局部收敛速度和数值稳定性的算法。

Method: 通过对原始变量进行尺度-形状分解（分离尺度与方向），构造了具有非奇异Jacobian的对偶问题。利用稳定的log-sum-exp和softmax原语评估目标和Jacobian，并使用Lambert W函数对尺度进行均匀界限控制，从而调控Jacobian的谱特性。算法采用阻尼非精确牛顿法实现全局线性收敛与局部超线性到二次收敛。

Result: 证明了算法具有全局线性收敛率（O(log ε⁻¹)迭代复杂度）和局部超线性到二次收敛速度；解映射关于数据、正则化参数和参考测度联合Lipschitz连续，且可连续延拓至零正则化极限。在量子蒙特卡洛数据解析延拓问题上的实验验证了抗溢出能力和收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该方法有效克服了有限精度溢出的数值困难，同时保证了全局和局部收敛速度，适用于高精度、大规模熵正则化最小二乘问题。

Abstract: We give a damped inexact Newton method for entropy-regularized least-squares on the nonnegative orthant that converges globally at a linear rate with $O(\logε^{-1})$ iteration complexity, locally at a superlinear-to-quadratic rate, and is immune to the finite-precision overflow that limits classical dual solvers. A scale-shape decomposition of the primal -- separating its scale from its direction -- produces a dual with a nonsingular Jacobian. Objectives and Jacobians are evaluated through stable log-sum-exp and softmax primitives. Lambert W bounds on the scale uniformly control the Jacobian's spectrum, from which both rates follow. The solution map is jointly Lipschitz in the data, regularization parameter, and reference measure, and extends continuously to the vanishing-regularization limit. Experiments on a problem from analytic continuation of quantum Monte Carlo data confirm the predicted overflow resilience and convergence behavior.

</details>


### [108] [Boundedness of solutions in feedback systems with antithetic controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27290)
*Moh Kamalul Wafi,Arthur C. B. de Oliveira,Eduardo D. Sontag*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一类非线性反馈系统的有界性，证明在合成生物学中由抗逆反馈控制器调节的时滞反馈系统所有轨迹均有界，关键在于时滞反馈最终能抵消状态的持续增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成生物学中抗逆反馈控制器通过时滞反馈环路调节生物过程，需要确保系统状态不会随时间无限增长，即保证系统有界性。

Method: 直接使用微分不等式进行证明，无需构造Lyapunov函数。核心思路是：当状态超过阈值并持续足够长时间时，反馈信号会变得足够强以迫使状态下降，且一旦下降，反馈将保持足够强度阻止状态无限增长。

Result: 证明了该非线性时滞反馈系统的所有轨迹均有界。该有界性结果可理解为时域小增益效应，即时滞反馈最终抵消系统中的任何持续增长。

Conclusion: 通过直接微分不等式方法建立了抗逆反馈控制系统的有界性，证明过程透明且易于解释，为合成生物学中此类系统的稳定性分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper studies whether solutions of a class of nonlinear feedback systems remain bounded over time. The systems we consider arise naturally in synthetic biology, where the antithetic feedback controller regulates a biological process through a delayed feedback loop. Our main result is that every trajectory of such a system is bounded. The key insight is simple: if the regulated state grows too large for too long, the feedback loop will eventually respond and push it back down. More precisely, we show that whenever the state exceeds a threshold and remains there long enough, the feedback signal becomes strong enough to force the state to decrease. We then show that once this happens, the feedback remains strong enough to keep the state from growing unbounded. The proof works directly with differential inequalities and does not require constructing a Lyapunov function, making the mechanism transparent and easy to interpret. The boundedness result can be understood as a time-domain small-gain effect, where the delayed feedback ultimately counteracts any persistent growth in the system.

</details>


### [109] [Over-Approximating Minimizer Sets of Constrained Convex Programs with Parametric Uncertainty via Reachability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27355)
*Brendan Gould,Chih-Yuan Chiu,Antoine P. Leeman,Kyriakos G. Vamvoudakis,Samuel Coogan,Glen Chou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对参数化强凸优化问题中不确定参数导致的最优解集，利用投影梯度下降（PGD）算法的收敛性，通过将迭代过程视为不确定动态系统并分析其前向可达集，构建了最优解集的可认证外逼近，且逼近误差随迭代次数指数衰减。通过系统级综合（SLS）优化步长序列，该方法在低保守性下显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化强凸优化问题中，成本函数依赖于不确定但有界的参数，导致最优解构成一个集合而非单一解。现有方法难以在低保守性下高效逼近高维决策变量的最优解集，因此需要一种可认证且保守性低的外逼近方法。

Method: 将成本参数视为常数但未知，将PGD迭代解释为不确定动态系统，通过分析其前向可达集来构建最优解集的外逼近；利用PGD对每个固定参数指数收敛到唯一最优解的特性，推导出显式的误差界；进一步采用系统级综合（SLS）技术优化PGD的步长序列，以减小可达集外逼近的保守性。

Result: 所提方法在逼近不确定成本凸规划的最优解集时，外逼近误差随迭代次数指数衰减，且在高维决策变量场景下显著优于现有基线方法，具有更低的保守性。

Conclusion: 基于PGD可达集的外逼近方法能够有效处理参数化强凸优化问题中的不确定性，结合SLS步长优化进一步降低了保守性，为高维不确定凸优化问题提供了一种高效、可认证的求解路径。

Abstract: We study the set of solutions to a parameterized, strongly convex optimization problem whose cost depends on uncertain, bounded parameters. We compute a certified outer approximation of the corresponding set of optimizers, using convergence properties of the projected gradient descent (PGD) algorithm for convex programs. Concretely, by treating the cost parameter as constant but unknown, we interpret the PGD iterates as an uncertain dynamical system and analyze its forward reachable sets. Since PGD converges exponentially to the unique optimizer for each fixed parameter, these reachable sets provide outer approximations of the optimizer set, with an explicit error bound that decays exponentially with the iteration count. We apply system-level synthesis (SLS) on the PGD dynamics to optimize the step-size sequence and obtain reachable-set over-approximations. Our method outperforms existing baselines in over-approximating, with low conservativeness, the minimizer sets of convex programs with uncertain costs and high-dimensional decision variables.

</details>


### [110] [Continuous-time q-learning for mean-field control with common noise, part-I: Theoretical foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27372)
*Zhenjie Ren,Xiaoli Wei,Xiang Yu,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带受控公共噪声的熵正则化平均场控制（MFC）的连续时间Q函数（q-function）。通过松弛控制公式推导出探索性HJB方程，在凹性条件下证明了最优单步策略迭代的存在唯一性，并引入集成q函数（Iq-function），在高斯策略下给出了线性二次型（LQ）问题的显式最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Jia和Zhou (2023)在单智能体模型中提出的q-function推广到带受控公共噪声的平均场控制场景，解决连续时间熵正则化MFC中的策略迭代与最优策略表征问题。

Method: 1) 建立离散采样动作下探索性公式与松弛控制公式值函数的收敛性；2) 推导含受控公共噪声的探索性HJB方程；3) 在凹性条件下利用策略的偏线性泛函导数通过一阶条件建立最优单步策略迭代的存在唯一性；4) 引入定义在状态分布和策略上的集成q函数（Iq-function），将最优策略识别为Iq-function argmax算子的两层不动点；5) 在LQ设定下给出最优策略的高斯分布显式刻画。

Result: 探索性公式与松弛控制公式的值函数在时间网格细化下收敛；满足凹性条件时，最优单步策略迭代存在且唯一；最优策略可表达为Iq-function argmax算子的两层不动点；在LQ设定下最优策略为高斯分布。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了带受控公共噪声的熵正则化MFC的q-function理论框架，为连续时间平均场控制中的策略优化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates the continuous-time counterpart of the Q-function for entropy-regularized mean-field control (MFC) with controlled common noise, coined as q-function by Jia and Zhou (2023) in the single agent's model. We first show that, under discretely sampled actions, the value function in the exploratory formulation converges to the one in the relaxed control formulation as the time grid refines. Leveraging the relaxed control formulation, we derive the exploratory Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equation, in which the controlled common noise gives rise to an additional nonlinear functional of policy, rendering the policy iteration intricate. Under certain concavity condition, we establish the existence and uniqueness of the optimal one-step policy iteration via a first-order condition using the partial linear functional derivative with respect to policy. The policy improvement at each iteration is verified by relating to an entropy-regularized optimization problem over the space of policies. In the mean-field setting, we introduce the integrated q-function (Iq-function) defined on the state distribution and the policy, and it is shown that an optimal policy is identified as a two-layer fixed point to the argmax operator of the Iq-function. Finally, we provide the explicit characterization of an optimal policy as a Gaussian distribution in the general linear-quadratic (LQ) setting.

</details>


### [111] [Continuous-time q-learning for mean-field control with common noise, part-II: q-learning algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27378)
*Zhenjie Ren,Xiaoli Wei,Xiang Yu,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文是Ren等人(2026)的延续工作，针对带受控共同噪声的平均场控制问题，基于松弛控制形式建立值函数和Iq-函数的鞅条件，量化可观测数据替代不可观测数据时的误差，结合双层不动点刻画提出Actor-Critic q-learning算法，并在无限时域线性二次型框架下证明内迭代收敛性，通过两个数值示例验证算法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续Ren等人(2026)的工作，进一步设计适用于带受控共同噪声的平均场控制问题的q-learning算法，解决松弛控制形式中数据不可观测导致的实际应用困难。

Method: 基于松弛控制形式建立鞅条件；量化从松弛形式到探索形式的数据替换误差；利用双层不动点刻画提出Actor-Critic q-learning算法（Actor步基于改进Iq-函数迭代更新策略，Critic步基于鞅正交条件更新值函数和Iq-函数）；在LQ框架下证明内迭代收敛性。

Result: 在无限时域LQ框架下建立了Actor步内迭代的收敛性；在LQ框架内外的两个数值示例中，所提q-learning算法均表现出满意的性能。

Conclusion: 本文成功将q-learning方法拓展至带受控共同噪声的MFC问题，通过数据替换误差量化和Actor-Critic架构设计解决了松弛控制形式的实际应用限制，理论与实验均验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: This paper is a continuation work of Ren et al. (2026) aiming to further devise q-learning algorithms for mean-field control (MFC) with controlled common noise. Based on the relaxed control formulation, we first establish the martingale condition of the value function and the Iq-function by evaluating along the conditional state distributions generated by all test policies. As the data in the relaxed control formulation are not observable in practice, we quantify the error incurred when they are replaced by the observable ones in the exploratory formulation under discretely sampled actions. This, together with a two-layer fixed point characterization of an optimal policy in Ren et al. (2026), allows us to propose several algorithms including the Actor-Critic q-learning algorithm, in which the policy is updated in the Actor-step based on the iteration rule induced by the improved Iq-function, and the value function and Iq-function are updated in the Critic-step based on the martingale orthogonality condition using the data from the exploratory formulation. We also establish the convergence of the inner iterations in the Actor-step in an infinite-horizon linear quadratic (LQ) framework. In two examples, within and beyond LQ framework, our q-learning algorithms are implemented with satisfactory performance.

</details>


### [112] [Mean-Field Systems with Heterogeneous Subteams: Optimality of Cluster-Symmetric Independent Policies and Equivalence with Decentralized McKean-Vlasov Control of Cluster-Representative Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27380)
*Connor S. Braun,Sina Sanjari,Naci Saldi,Gunnar Blohm,Serdar Yüksel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有有限异质性的多智能体系统，通过推广De Finetti定理，证明了在无限人口极限下，最优联合策略收敛到去中心化的、簇内对称的策略，并建立了McKean-Vlasov团队表示及验证定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统均场方法通常假设所有智能体同质（对称/均匀），这在建模上过于严格。本文旨在突破这一限制，在有限异质性条件下为大规模交互粒子系统提供理论分析框架。

Method: 考虑由有限个物种（簇）组成的离散时间团队，利用部分可交换代价准则；通过推广De Finetti定理证明最优策略在无限人口极限下收敛到去中心化策略；建立McKean-Vlasov团队表示及验证定理/值迭代。

Result: 证明了中心化联合策略的最优性，该策略在簇内可交换且仅依赖于各簇的经验分布；推广的De Finetti定理表明最优策略序列收敛到去中心化、簇内对称的策略；该极限策略诱导出对有限人口问题渐近最优的策略序列。

Conclusion: 为分析具有有限异质性的复杂合作系统提供了理论途径，并为后续学习算法的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Across science and engineering, mean-field methods have been a powerful and versatile approach for the analysis of systems of many interacting elements. However, common arguments used to characterize an infinite population limit can be quite restrictive from a modeling perspective by requiring that all agents be identical (i.e. symmetric, or homogeneous). In this paper, we consider large interactive particle systems under agent heterogeneity for a class of discrete time teams composed of finitely many species of agents, grouped into symmetric subteams, called clusters. In particular, for the class of discounted, partially exchangeable cost criteria considered, we establish the optimality of centralized joint policies which are exchangeable within each cluster and depend on the agent ensemble only up to the state empirical distribution over each cluster. Following this, a generalization of De Finetti's theorem is used to demonstrate the subsequential convergence of these optimal policies to one which is decentralized (depending on only the local state and distribution over each cluster) and symmetric within each subteam as the population size approaches infinity. This solution is shown to induce a sequence of asymptotically optimal policies for the finite population problems which retain their structure and decentralization. Furthermore, our analysis justifies the optimality of a decentralized McKean-Vlasov team representation involving coupled representative agents for each of the clusters, and establishes a verification theorem/value iterations for the mean-field limit. In this way, we provide an avenue for analyzing complex, cooperative systems with finite heterogeneity and set the stage for further research on learning algorithms.

</details>


### [113] [A Regularized Hessian-Free Inexact Newton-Type Method with Global $\mathcal{O}(k^{-2})$ Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27406)
*Leandro Farias Maia,Antonio Victor B. Nascimento,Paulo Sergio M. Santos,Gilson N. Silva*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种正则化的无海森牛顿型方法，用于最小化具有Lipschitz连续海森矩阵的光滑凸函数。该方法通过有限差分构造近似海森矩阵，并采用自适应准则选择正则化参数，保证了充分下降和梯度控制。证明了全局收敛率为O(k^{-2})，匹配二阶方法的最佳已知界。在标准假设下，使用精确海森矩阵的改进变体具有局部二次收敛性。在多个凸基准问题上，该变体的计算速度比Mishchenko (2023)的正则化牛顿法更快。提供了正则化序列的显式界和最坏情况迭代复杂度O(ε^{-2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一正则化牛顿法和无海森牛顿型方法，在保证理论收敛性的同时提高实际计算效率。

Method: 采用有限差分构造近似海森矩阵，通过自适应准则选择正则化参数，结合无海森技术和正则化技术。

Result: 全局收敛率O(k^{-2})，局部二次收敛，最坏情况迭代复杂度O(ε^{-2})，在凸基准问题上计算速度快于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了正则化与无海森牛顿型方案，为光滑凸优化提供了理论上可靠且实践中高效的替代方案。

Abstract: We propose a regularized Hessian-free Newton-type method for minimizing smooth convex functions with Lipschitz continuous Hessians. The algorithm constructs an approximate Hessian by finite differences and selects the regularization parameter through an adaptive criterion that ensures sufficient decrease and gradient control. We prove that the method achieves a global $\mathcal{O}(k^{-2})$ convergence rate, matching the best known bound for second-order methods. A modified variant incorporating the exact Hessian when available enjoys local quadratic convergence under standard assumptions. Despite its simplicity, this variant is computationally faster than the \emph{Regularized Newton Method} of Mishchenko (2023) across several convex benchmark problems. Our analysis also provides explicit bounds on the regularization sequence and a worst-case iteration complexity of order $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$. The proposed framework thus unifies regularized and Hessian-free Newton-type schemes, offering a theoretically sound and practically efficient alternative for smooth convex optimization.

</details>


### [114] [A Geometric Perspective on Polynomially Solvable Convex Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27427)
*Shaoning Han,Liangju Li,Yongchun Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出共单调性（comonotonicity）这一可行域结构性质，并在此条件下建立了固定秩凸最大化的统一枚举框架，证明其多项式可解性。该框架统一了多个已有结果（如固定秩凸拟阵最大化、稀疏主成分分析），并通过提升技术对标准共单调可行域实现了复杂度平方根改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸最大化问题即使在低秩目标下通常是NP难的，本文旨在探索在何种结构条件下该问题可以多项式时间求解。

Method: 从几何角度引入共单调性概念刻画可行域结构，建立该性质的数学刻画；在此性质及温和附加假设下，提出统一的枚举框架；针对标准共单调可行域，进一步采用提升技术优化复杂度。

Result: 证明在共单调性条件下，固定秩凸最大化问题是多项式可解的；该框架统一了固定秩凸拟阵最大化和稀疏主成分分析等已知结果；对标准共单调可行域实现了复杂度平方根改进。

Conclusion: 共单调性是凸最大化问题多项式可解的关键结构性质，所提出的统一框架具有广泛适用性，在稀疏主成分分析及其变体等应用中效果显著。

Abstract: Convex maximization encompasses a broad class of optimization problems and is generally NP-hard, even for low-rank objectives. This paper investigates structural conditions under which convex maximization becomes polynomially solvable. From a geometric perspective, we introduce comonotonicity, a structural property of the feasible region crucial for problem tractability, and establish mathematical characterizations of this property. Under comonotonicity and mild additional assumptions, we develop a unified enumerative framework showing that fixed-rank convex maximization is polynomially solvable. This viewpoint recovers several known tractability results that previously required separate analyses, such as fixed-rank convex matroid maximization and sparse principal component analysis (SPCA). Furthermore, for the more structured class of standard comonotone feasible regions, we refine the analysis via a lifting technique to achieve a square-root improvement in the complexity bound. Finally, applications to SPCA and its variants illustrate the broad applicability and effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [115] [Sampler-Robust Optimization under Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27447)
*Ziwei Zhang,Jonathan Yu-Meng Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出采样器鲁棒优化（SRO）框架，通过扰动生成模型诱导的采样器来优化决策，同时抵御采样器误规范和有限模拟误差，在覆盖假设下证明经验最坏情况目标为真实总体目标提供高概率上界，并在投资组合优化中展现出更稳定的决策和更好的分布外表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代随机优化流程依赖学习生成模型表示不确定性，但下游决策几乎完全通过蒙特卡洛场景评估。不确定性操作对象从显式概率律转向生成器诱导的采样器，可靠性受制于采样器误规范和有限模拟误差，亟需一种直接与模拟决策流程对齐的鲁棒优化方法。

Method: 提出采样器鲁棒优化（SRO），在扰动学习生成器诱导的采样器集合上优化最坏情况下的决策目标。该框架采用采样器优先的公式，与基于模拟的决策流程一致，并具有锐度感知解释——偏好那些在生成器扰动下性能稳定而非仅在名义采样器下稳定的决策。框架兼容有/无显式密度的生成模型，并支持高效的极小极大程序。

Result: 在覆盖假设下，证明了经验最坏情况目标为真实总体目标提供高概率上界证书，且有限模拟误差被部分吸收到用于抵御采样器误规范的鲁棒化中。投资组合优化实验表明SRO产生更稳定的决策，并在分布偏移下改善样本外表现。

Conclusion: 采样器鲁棒优化为基于模拟的随机优化提供了一种理论保证且实用的鲁棒决策框架，有效结合了采样器误规范和有限模拟误差的防护。

Abstract: Modern stochastic optimization pipelines increasingly rely on learned generative models to represent uncertainty, while downstream decisions are evaluated almost entirely through Monte Carlo scenarios. This shifts the operational object of uncertainty from an explicit probability law to the sampler induced by the learned generator. Reliability therefore depends on two errors: sampler misspecification and finite-simulation error. We propose Sampler-Robust Optimization (SRO), which optimizes decisions against the worst-case sampler induced by perturbing the learned generator. This sampler-first formulation aligns with simulation-based decision pipelines and admits a sharpness-aware interpretation: it favors decisions whose performance is stable under generator perturbations, rather than merely under the nominal sampler. Under a coverage assumption, we show that the empirical worst-case objective provides a high-probability upper certificate for the true population objective, with finite-simulation error partially absorbed by the robustification used to guard against sampler misspecification. The framework accommodates generative models with or without explicit densities and admits efficient minimax procedures. Portfolio-optimization experiments show that SRO produces more stable decisions and improves out-of-sample performance under distribution shift.

</details>


### [116] [Solution Sets for Inverse Infinite-Horizon Linear-Quadratic Descriptor Differential Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27460)
*Aaditya Kumar,Puduru Viswanadha Reddy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限时域线性二次微分对策的模型逆问题，旨在识别能够将观测到的反馈策略剖面解释为反馈纳什均衡的所有成本函数集合（解集），并证明该集合是矩形且凸的，给出了计算可行实现的算法及数值示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决带有描述子动力学的无限时域线性二次微分对策中的模型逆问题，从观测到的反馈策略剖面逆向识别合理的成本函数集合。

Method: 对解集进行理论刻画，证明其矩形性和凸性，并设计算法计算一个可行实现。

Result: 解集具有矩形性和凸性，算法能够计算出一个可行的成本函数实现。

Conclusion: 通过数值示例验证了所提方法的有效性，为逆强化学习在微分对策中的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this letter, we study a model-based inverse problem for infinite-horizon linear-quadratic differential games with descriptor dynamics. Specifically, we seek to identify the set of all cost functions that rationalize an observed feedback strategy profile of the players as a feedback Nash equilibrium, referred to here as the solution set. We characterize the solution set, show that it is rectangular and convex, and provide an algorithm to compute an admissible realization. Finally, we illustrate our results with numerical examples.

</details>


### [117] [Robust Constrained Optimization via Sliding Mode Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27587)
*Shyam Kamal,Baby Diana,Sunidhi Pandey,Sandip Ghosh,Thach Ngoc Dinh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于滑模控制的等式约束优化框架，通过将KKT条件重构为控制仿射系统，将优化变量视为状态、拉格朗日乘子视为控制输入、等式约束定义为滑模面，实现了有限时间精确约束满足和目标收敛，且不依赖目标凸性，对匹配扰动、结构不确定性和有界测量噪声具有鲁棒性。引入非奇异终端滑模范数梯度流加速收敛，Lyapunov分析证明了闭环稳定性与收敛性，数值实验验证了优越的精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间优化方法在处理等式约束时往往收敛速度慢、对扰动敏感，且依赖目标函数的凸性假设。本文旨在开发一种不依赖凸性、具有强鲁棒性且能有限时间精确满足约束的优化框架。

Method: 将等式约束优化的KKT一阶条件重构为控制仿射动力系统，优化变量为状态，拉格朗日乘子为控制输入，等式约束作为滑模面；采用滑模控制设计保证有限时间精确约束满足；引入非奇异终端滑模范数梯度流加速最优解收敛；通过Lyapunov理论进行稳定性与收敛性分析。

Result: 提出的方法在多种基准问题上相比经典连续时间优化方法展现出更高的精度和更强的鲁棒性，特别在存在扰动的情况下效果显著。

Conclusion: 基于滑模控制的等式约束优化框架能够在不依赖凸性的条件下实现有限时间精确约束满足和目标收敛，并对外部扰动具有强鲁棒性，是一种有效的连续时间优化新范式。

Abstract: This paper develops a sliding mode control based frame work for equality constrained optimization by reformulation the first order Karush Kuhn Tucker conditions as control affine dynamical system. The optimization variables are treated as states and the Lagrange multipliers as control input, with equality constraints defined as sliding manifold. The resulting design guarantees exact constraint enforcement with finite time convergence, independent of objective convexity, and exhibits robustness to matched disturbance, structural uncertainty and bounded measurement noise. To accelerate the convergence, a nonsingular terminal sliding mode based normed gradient flow is introduced, ensuring both finite time convergence to optimal solution and constraint satisfaction. Rigorous Lyapunov analysis establishes closed loop stability and convergence. Numerical studies across diverse benchmark problems demonstrate superior accuracy and robustness over classical continuous time optimization method, highlighting effectiveness under disturbance.

</details>


### [118] [Data-Driven Continuous-Time Linear Quadratic Regulator via Closed-Loop and Reinforcement Learning Parameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27922)
*Armin Gießler,Felix Thömmes,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间线性二次型调节器(LQR)问题的两种数据驱动参数化方法：闭环(CL)参数化和积分强化学习(IRL)参数化，分别推导了策略迭代方案、数据驱动CARE方程、策略梯度流和凸优化重构，并提供了统一框架以阐明二者结构关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动LQR方法主要针对离散时间系统，连续时间系统的研究相对不足，且两种主流参数化方法(CL和IRL)之间的关系尚未被系统阐明。

Method: (1) 将CL参数化从离散时间推广到连续时间，建立矩阵等式约束，推导数据驱动CARE方程和策略迭代方案；(2) 在IRL框架下利用离策略数据进行策略评估，推导策略梯度流；(3) 提出两种参数化的凸优化重构；(4) 统一分析CL与IRL的结构关系。

Result: 成功将CL参数化推广至连续时间，建立了连续时间数据驱动CARE方程；在IRL框架下推导了策略梯度流；提出了两种参数化的凸优化形式；揭示了CL与IRL参数化之间的结构等价性和互补性。

Conclusion: 两种数据驱动参数化方法在连续时间LQR问题中各有优势：CL参数化适合闭环系统辨识，IRL参数化适合离策略学习；统一框架有助于根据实际问题选择合适方法，并为后续数据驱动控制理论奠定基础。

Abstract: This paper studies data-driven approaches to the continuous-time linear quadratic regulator (LQR) problem based on two existing parameterizations, namely a closed-loop (CL) parameterization from behavioral system theory and an integral reinforcement learning (IRL) parameterization. The CL parameterization characterizes the closed-loop system via a matrix that satisfies equality constraints. While this parameterization has been extensively studied for discrete-time systems, we adapt key results to the continuous-time setting and develop a policy iteration (PI) scheme, derive a data-driven continuous-time algebraic Riccati equation (CARE), and introduce an alternative convex problem formulation. The IRL parameterization utilizes off-policy data to perform policy evaluation, which is then used for PI or value iteration. Within the IRL framework, we derive a policy gradient flow and propose convex reformulations of the LQR problem. Finally, we provide a unified treatment of these parameterizations that enables a systematic understanding of existing approaches and clarifies their structural relationships.

</details>


### [119] [Frank-Wolfe Beyond 1/t Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28006)
*Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出局部对偶尖锐性（LDS）条件，证明在该条件下Frank-Wolfe算法对任何光滑凸函数都能实现$o(1/t)$收敛，突破了传统$Ω(1/t)$的下界，并给出了均匀凸集上的首个无条件$o(1/t)$收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Frank-Wolfe算法在一般光滑凸情形下存在$Ω(1/t)$的不可逾越下界，更快的收敛通常需要强凸性或Hölder误差界等有利函数性质。本文旨在探索是否仅凭可行区域本身的几何性质就能突破这一下界，而无需对目标函数施加额外限制。

Method: 提出局部对偶尖锐性（LDS）条件——一种可行区域及其线性最小化（LMO）的几何性质，是集合均匀凸性的推广与局部化。在该条件下，利用Frank-Wolfe算法的对偶间隙与原始间隙之间的关系，通过精细的迭代分析证明无条件$o(1/t)$收敛。进一步将LDS与局部Hölder误差界结合，导出量化收敛速率。

Result: 1) 证明在LDS条件下，Frank-Wolfe算法对任意光滑凸函数收敛速度为$o(1/t)$，排除了$Ω(1/t)$的下界。2) 证明任何均匀凸集都满足LDS条件，给出均匀凸集上首个无条件$o(1/t)$收敛结果。3) 结合LDS与局部Hölder误差界可进一步量化具体收敛速率。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了可行区域的几何性质（LDS）本身足以突破Frank-Wolfe算法的一般收敛下界，将加速收敛的条件从函数性质扩展到了区域几何性质。该工作为理解凸优化中算法收敛性的几何本质提供了新视角，且LDS条件比均匀凸性更广泛，具有更一般的适用性。

Abstract: We consider smooth convex minimization over compact convex sets, i.e., $\min_{x \in C} f(x)$ with the (vanilla) Frank-Wolfe algorithm. Well-known lower bounds establish a worst-case $Ω(1/t)$ primal-gap barrier in the general smooth convex case, and faster convergence usually requires favorable function properties such as Hölder error bounds or strong convexity. We present a new Local Dual Sharpness (LDS) condition, essentially a property of the feasible region and its LMO, under which the Frank-Wolfe algorithm converges in $o(1/t)$ for any smooth convex function, ruling out an $Ω(1/t)$ lower bound under LDS. The condition is a generalization (and localization) of uniform convexity of sets and it is satisfied by any uniformly convex set. To our knowledge, this is the first unconditional $o(1/t)$ convergence result for uniformly convex sets. Combining LDS with stronger function properties, e.g., a local variant of Hölder error bounds, allows us to quantify the actual rates.

</details>


### [120] [A Scaled Gradient Modified Non-monotone Line Search Method for Constrained Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28110)
*Qamrul Hasan Ansari,Feeroz Babu,D. R. Sahu,Jen Chih Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于求解约束最小化问题的缩放梯度修正非单调线搜索方法，证明了该方法在目标函数为强拟凸时的线性收敛率，并通过大规模分式规划和二次规划数值实验验证了其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的约束最小化求解方法在处理大规模问题时存在收敛速度慢或单调线搜索的局限性，需要一种具有更强收敛保证且适用于非凸（如强拟凸、伪凸）目标函数的高效算法。

Method: 提出了一种缩放梯度修正非单调线搜索方法，结合了缩放梯度技巧与非单调线搜索策略，并在强拟凸假设下建立了线性收敛率分析，数值实验中与现有方法进行对比。

Result: 理论分析证明了算法在强拟凸目标函数下的线性收敛性；大规模分式规划和二次规划的数值实验表明，该算法相比现有方法具有更优的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的缩放梯度修正非单调线搜索方法在求解含强拟凸/伪凸目标函数的约束最小化问题时，理论上有收敛保证，实践上比现有方法更高效，适用于大规模优化场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a scaled gradient modified non-monotone line search method for solving constrained minimization problems, and explore several specific properties of this method, namely, its convergence analysis. We discuss the linear convergence rate of the sequence generated by the proposed algorithm to a solution of the constrained minimization problem where the objective function is strongly quasiconvex. We consider numerical examples of large-scale fractional programming and quadratic programming for the function of pseudo convex and strongly quasiconvex and compare the performance of the proposed algorithm with the existing ones for these examples.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [When Your LLM Reaches End-of-Life: A Framework for Confident Model Migration in Production Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27082)
*Emma Casey,David Roberts,David Sim,Ian Beaver*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于贝叶斯统计的框架，用于在生产环境中迁移大语言模型（LLM），通过将自动评估指标与人工判断进行校准，在有限人工评估数据下实现模型间的可靠比较。该框架在服务530万月交互量的商业问答系统中得到验证，可有效评估正确性、拒绝行为和风格一致性，适用于任何部署LLM产品的企业。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当底层LLM到达生命周期终点或需要替换时，生产系统面临模型迁移挑战。现有方法缺乏一种原则化、可重复的评估流程，在有限人工标注数据下难以做出可靠的模型替换决策。

Method: 采用贝叶斯统计方法，将自动评估指标与人工判断进行校准，从而在仅拥有少量人工评估数据的情况下，仍能对候选模型进行有置信度的比较。

Result: 在覆盖六个全球区域、月交互量达530万次的商业问答系统中成功验证，能够有效评估模型的正确性、拒绝行为和风格一致性，并识别出合适的替换模型。

Conclusion: 该框架广泛适用于任何部署LLM产品的企业，提供了一种可重复、原则化的模型迁移方法论，能够在保证质量的同时提升评估效率，在LLM生态快速演变的背景下具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: We present a framework for migrating production Large Language Model (LLM) based systems when the underlying model reaches end-of-life or requires replacement. The key contribution is a Bayesian statistical approach that calibrates automated evaluation metrics against human judgments, enabling confident model comparison even with limited manual evaluation data. We demonstrate this framework on a commercial question-answering system serving 5.3M monthly interactions across six global regions; evaluating correctness, refusal behavior, and stylistic adherence to successfully identify suitable replacement models. The framework is broadly applicable to any enterprise deploying LLM-based products, providing a principled, reproducible methodology for model migration that balances quality assurance with evaluation efficiency. This is a capability increasingly essential as the LLM ecosystem continues to evolve rapidly and organizations manage portfolios of AI-powered services across multiple models, regions, and use cases.

</details>


### [122] [End-to-end autonomous scientific discovery on a real optical platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27092)
*Shuxing Yang,Fujia Chen,Rui Zhao,Junyao Wu,Yize Wang,Haiyao Luo,Ning Han,Qiaolu Chen,Yuze Hu,Wenhao Li,Mingzhu Li,Hongsheng Chen,Yihao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Qiushi Discovery Engine——一个基于大语言模型的智能体系统，能够在真实光学平台上实现端到端的自主科学发现。该系统自主复现了已发表的透射矩阵实验、将抽象相干阶理论转化为实验可观测量，并在开放式研究中自主提出并实验验证了光学双线性相互作用——一种与Transformer注意力机制核心操作结构类似的物理机制。这是首次展示AI智能体系统能够自主识别并实验验证一个非平凡、此前未报告的物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现长期以来由人类主导，虽然基于LLM的智能体开始超越辅助预设研究流程的角色，但尚未有系统能在真实物理系统中实现端到端自主发现并产生由实验证据支持的非平凡结果。填补这一空白是本文的核心动机。

Method: Qiushi Engine采用非线性研究阶段、Meta-Trace记忆和双层架构，在涉及数千次LLM推理、测量和修正动作的长周期研究中维持自适应且稳定的研究轨迹。系统在真实光学平台上运行，自主执行端到端科学发现流程。

Result: 系统自主复现了已发表的透射矩阵实验，将抽象相干阶理论转化为实验观测（首次观测该类结构）。在开放研究中消耗1.459亿token、3,242次LLM调用、1,242次工具调用、163份研究笔记和44个脚本，提出并实验验证了光学双线性相互作用——一种与Transformer注意力机制核心操作结构类似的物理机制。

Conclusion: 这是首个展示AI智能体系统自主识别并实验验证非平凡、此前未报告物理机制的工作，标志着自主智能体在研究级别上的里程碑。AI发现的机制为高速、节能的光学硬件用于成对计算提供了新路径。

Abstract: Scientific research has long been human-led, driving new knowledge and transformative technologies through the continual revision of questions, methods and claims as evidence accumulates. Although large language model (LLM)-based agents are beginning to move beyond assisting predefined research workflows, none has yet demonstrated end-to-end autonomous discovery in a real physical system that produces a nontrivial result supported by experimental evidence. Here we introduce Qiushi Discovery Engine, an LLM-based agentic system for end-to-end autonomous scientific discovery on a real optical platform. Qiushi Engine combines nonlinear research phases, Meta-Trace memory and a dual-layer architecture to maintain adaptive and stable research trajectories across long-horizon investigations involving thousands of LLM-mediated reasoning, measurement and revision actions. It autonomously reproduces a published transmission-matrix experiment on a non-original platform and converts an abstract coherence-order theory into experimental observables, providing, to our knowledge, the first observation of this class of coherence-order structure. More importantly, in an open-ended study involving 145.9 million tokens, 3,242 LLM calls, 1,242 tool calls, 163 research notes and 44 scripts, Qiushi Engine proposes and experimentally validates optical bilinear interaction, a physical mechanism structurally analogous to a core operation in Transformer attention. This AI-discovered mechanism suggests a route towards high-speed, energy-efficient optical hardware for pairwise computation. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of an AI agentic system autonomously identifying and experimentally validating a nontrivial, previously unreported physical mechanism, marking a milestone for research-level autonomous agents.

</details>


### [123] [Think it, Run it: Autonomous ML pipeline generation via self-healing multi-agent AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27096)
*Adela Bara,Gabriela Dobrita,Simona-Vasilica Oprea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个五智能体统一架构，通过代码接地RAG、可解释混合推荐、自愈机制和自适应学习，实现从数据集和自然语言目标到端到端ML管道的自动生成，在150个ML任务上达到84.7%的成功率，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML管道生成方法缺乏统一的端到端自动化架构，难以同时保证效率、鲁棒性和可解释性，且各组件（如推荐、执行、错误处理）多为孤立解决方案，缺乏紧密耦合的智能协作机制。

Method: 提出五智能体系统，分别负责数据剖析、意图解析、微服务推荐、有向无环图(DAG)构建与执行。集成代码接地RAG增强对微服务的理解，设计结合多准则的可解释混合推荐器，并引入基于LLM错误解释的自愈机制及执行历史自适应学习。

Result: 在150个多样化ML任务上评估，端到端管道成功率达84.7%，优于基线方法；自愈机制显著提升鲁棒性，且相比人工构建大幅缩短工作流开发时间。

Conclusion: 紧密耦合的智能组件架构能够超越孤立解决方案，代码接地RAG、可解释推荐、自愈执行与自适应学习的创新集成证明了统一多智能体架构在自动化ML管道生成中的有效性。

Abstract: The purpose of our paper is to develop a unified multi-agent architecture that automates end-to-end machine learning (ML) pipeline generation from datasets and natural-language (NL) goals, improving efficiency, robustness and explainability. A five-agent system is proposed to handle profiling, intent parsing, microservice recommendation, Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) construction and execution. It integrates code-grounded Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) for microservice understanding, an explainable hybrid recommender combining multiple criteria, a self-healing mechanism using Large Language Model (LLM)-based error interpretation and adaptive learning from execution history. The approach is evaluated on 150 ML tasks across diverse scenarios. The system achieves an 84.7% end-to-end pipeline success rate, outperforming baseline methods. It demonstrates improved robustness through self-healing and reduces workflow development time compared to manual construction. The study introduces a novel integration of code-grounded RAG, explainable recommendation, self-healing execution and adaptive learning within a single architecture, showing that tightly coupled intelligent components can outperform isolated solutions.

</details>


### [124] [Unsupervised Electrofacies Classification and Porosity Characterization in the Offshore Keta Basin Using Wireline Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27126)
*Hamdiya Adams,Theophilus Ansah-Narh,Daniel Kwadwo Asiedu,Bruce Kofi Banoeng-Yakubo,Marcellin Atemkeng,Thomas Armah,Richmond Opoku-Sarkodie,Rebecca Davis,Ezekiel Nii Noye Nortey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究针对加纳近海Keta盆地岩心数据稀缺的问题，提出了一种无监督机器学习工作流进行电相分析。通过对Well~C井约11,195个样本的六种标准测井数据进行K-means聚类，结合惯性和轮廓系数诊断，识别出四个电相簇（平均轮廓系数约0.50），这些电相呈现出从页岩主导到清洁砂岩主导的连续地质变化，证明了仅依赖测井数据的无监督聚类方法在早期盆地评估中的鲁棒性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近海Keta盆地岩心数据稀缺，传统基于岩心的储层表征方法受限，亟需一种仅依赖测井数据的无监督学习方法进行电相分析，以支持早期勘探阶段的储层评价。

Method: 采用无监督机器学习工作流：对Well~C井约11,195个样本的六种标准测井数据进行K-means聚类，使用惯性和轮廓诊断评估聚类结构，最终识别出四个电相簇。

Result: 识别出四个电相簇，平均轮廓系数约0.50，表明聚类具有中等但有意义的分离度；电相呈现从页岩到清洁砂岩的深度连续模式，与黏土含量、孔隙度和岩石骨架性质变化相关。

Conclusion: 仅依赖测井数据的无监督聚类方法，辅以定量指标支持，为近海前沿盆地的早期地层评价提供了稳健、可重复的框架，并为未来综合研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: This study presents an unsupervised machine learning workflow for electrofacies analysis in the offshore Keta Basin, Ghana, where core data are scarce. Six standard wireline logs from Well~C were analysed over a depth interval comprising approximately $11{,}195$ samples. K-means clustering was applied in multivariate log space, with the clustering structure evaluated using inertia and silhouette diagnostics. Four clusters were identified, supported by an average silhouette coefficient of approximately $0.50$, indicating moderate but meaningful separation. The resulting electrofacies exhibit systematic, depth-continuous patterns associated with variations in clay content, porosity, and rock framework properties, forming a geological continuum from shale-dominated to cleaner sandstone-dominated units. The results demonstrate that log-only, unsupervised clustering supported by quantitative metrics provides a robust and reproducible framework for subsurface characterisation. The proposed workflow offers a practical tool for early-stage formation evaluation in frontier offshore basins and a foundation for future integrated studies.

</details>


### [125] [TRUST: A Framework for Decentralized AI Service v.0.1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27132)
*Yu-Chao Huang,Zhen Tan,Mohan Zhang,Pingzhi Li,Zhuo Zhang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRUST框架，一种用于大规模推理模型和多智能体系统的去中心化审计框架，通过HDAG、DAAN协议和多层级共识机制，在30%对抗参与者下保证正确性，在多个基准测试中准确率达72.4%，比基线提升4-18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模推理模型和多智能体系统在高风险领域需要可靠的验证机制，但现有的中心化审计方法存在鲁棒性差（单点故障）、可扩展性不足（推理复杂度导致瓶颈）、不透明性（隐藏审计侵蚀信任）和隐私泄露（暴露推理轨迹导致模型盗窃）四个关键局限。

Method: TRUST提出三项核心创新：(1) 层次化有向无环图(HDAG)，将链式思维推理分解为五个抽象层级以支持并行分布式审计；(2) DAAN协议，将多智能体交互投影为因果交互图(CIG)以实现确定性根因归因；(3) 多层级共识机制，结合计算检查器、LLM评估器和人类专家，采用权益加权投票，在30%对抗参与者存在下保证正确性。此外，通过隐私保护设计分割防止专有逻辑重建，所有决策记录在链上。

Result: 在多个LLM和基准测试中，TRUST达到72.4%准确率（比基线高4-18%），在20%腐败率下保持韧性。DAAN达到70%根因归因准确率（标准方法为54-63%），节省60%token。人工验证F1=0.89，Brier=0.074。

Conclusion: TRUST框架支持去中心化审计、防篡改排行榜、去信任数据标注和受控自主智能体四种应用场景，开创了推理能力系统安全、可问责部署的去中心化AI审计范式。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) and Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) in high-stakes domains demand reliable verification, yet centralized approaches suffer four limitations: (1) Robustness, with single points of failure vulnerable to attacks and bias; (2) Scalability, as reasoning complexity creates bottlenecks; (3) Opacity, as hidden auditing erodes trust; and (4) Privacy, as exposed reasoning traces risk model theft. We introduce TRUST (Transparent, Robust, and Unified Services for Trustworthy AI), a decentralized framework with three innovations: (i) Hierarchical Directed Acyclic Graphs (HDAGs) that decompose Chain-of-Thought reasoning into five abstraction levels for parallel distributed auditing; (ii) the DAAN protocol, which projects multi-agent interactions into Causal Interaction Graphs (CIGs) for deterministic root-cause attribution; and (iii) a multi-tier consensus mechanism among computational checkers, LLM evaluators, and human experts with stake-weighted voting that guarantees correctness under 30% adversarial participation. We prove a Safety-Profitability Theorem ensuring honest auditors profit while malicious actors incur losses. All decisions are recorded on-chain, while privacy-by-design segmentation prevents reconstruction of proprietary logic. Across multiple LLMs and benchmarks, TRUST attains 72.4% accuracy (4-18% above baselines) and remains resilient against 20% corruption. DAAN reaches 70% root-cause attribution (vs. 54-63% for standard methods) with 60% token savings. Human studies validate the design (F1 = 0.89, Brier = 0.074). The framework supports (A1) decentralized auditing, (A2) tamper-proof leaderboards, (A3) trustless data annotation, and (A4) governed autonomous agents, pioneering decentralized AI auditing for safe, accountable deployment of reasoning-capable systems.

</details>


### [126] [Optimal Stop-Loss and Take-Profit Parameterization for Autonomous Trading Agent Swarm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27150)
*Nathan Li,Aikins Laryea,Yigit Ihlamur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在自主加密货币交易系统中，使用更优的止损和止盈设置能否提升交易智能体群体的表现。基于900多笔历史交易，通过回放不同退出策略对比发现，更严格的损失限制、更早的获利了结和更紧密的追踪保护能显著改善风险调整后收益。同时指出时间顺序分割评估可能被异常市场时期扭曲，采用随机化数据作为主要对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主加密交易系统大多将设计精力放在入场策略上，而退出（止损/止盈）往往依赖未经系统测试的固定规则。论文旨在弥补这一空白，系统检验退出策略设计对交易性能的影响。

Method: 基于900多笔历史交易数据，对每笔交易在多种备选退出策略下进行回放，对比结果与现有生产环境设置。评估中对比了纯时间顺序分割与随机化数据两种方式，以应对异常市场时期对结果的扭曲。

Result: 退出设计对交易表现有显著影响：更优的配置能改善风险调整后收益，且普遍倾向于更紧的止损限制、更早的获利了结以及更紧密的追踪保护。时间顺序分割评估因最新交易落入战争驱动的异常市场时期而严重失真，随机化数据减少了这一单次事件的影响。

Conclusion: 论文提供了一个实用框架，用于以更规范、更透明的方式调优交易系统的退出逻辑，强调了退出策略设计的重要性并指出了评估方法中可能存在的陷阱。

Abstract: Autonomous crypto trading systems often spend most of their design effort on finding entries, while exits are left to fixed rules that are rarely tested in a systematic way. This paper examines whether better stop-loss and take-profit settings can improve the performance of an autonomous trading agent swarm. Using more than 900 historical trades, we replay each trade under many alternative exit policies and compare results against the existing production setup. The study finds that exit design matters meaningfully: stronger configurations improve risk-adjusted performance and generally favor tighter loss limits, earlier profit capture, and closer trailing protection. The paper also discusses a key evaluation challenge: a purely chronological split was initially used, but the newest trades fell into an unusual war-driven market period that sharply distorted test results. To reduce the influence of that single episode, the main comparison was run on randomized data, with the drawbacks of doing so acknowledged explicitly. Overall, the paper presents a practical framework for tuning exit logic in a more disciplined and transparent way.

</details>


### [127] [Toward Personalized Digital Twins for Cognitive Decline Assessment: A Multimodal, Uncertainty-Aware Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27217)
*Bulent Soykan,Gulsah Hancerliogullari Koksalmis,Hsin-Hsiung Huang,Laura J. Brattain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种个性化认知衰退评估数字孪生框架（PCD-DT），结合隐状态空间模型、多模态融合和不确定性感知验证，用于从稀疏、噪声、不规则纵向数据中建模患者特异性神经退行性疾病轨迹。在TADPOLE数据集上的初步可行性实验表明，结合认知评分与MRI的多模态配置在下次就诊预测中取得了最低标准化RMSE（ADAS13: 0.4419，脑室体积: 0.5842），优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知衰退在不同个体间高度异质，这给预后判断、临床试验设计和治疗方案制定带来了困难。现有方法难以从稀疏、不规则且含噪声的纵向数据中有效捕捉个体化疾病轨迹。

Method: 提出PCD-DT框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 隐状态空间模型用于个体化时序动态建模；(2) 多模态融合整合临床、生物标志物和影像特征；(3) 不确定性感知的验证与自适应更新机制。同时引入条件生成模型支持数据增强和压力测试。实验采用LSTM序列模型在TADPOLE数据集上进行多模态下次就诊预测消融研究。

Result: TADPOLE轨迹分析显示ADAS13、脑室体积和海马体积在认知正常与阿尔茨海默病队列间存在清晰分离；多模态消融实验中，认知评分+MRI配置取得了ADAS13（标准化RMSE: 0.4419）和脑室体积（0.5842）的最低误差，优于Last Observation Carried Forward基线。

Conclusion: PCD-DT框架为神经退行性疾病个性化计算机模拟建模提供了一个有原则的起点，但需要更强的不确定性校准和更长预测视野的评估，才能迈向临床可部署的数字孪生系统。

Abstract: Cognitive decline is highly heterogeneous across individuals, which complicates prognosis, trial design, and treatment planning. We present the Personalized Cognitive Decline Assessment Digital Twin (PCD-DT), a multimodal and uncertainty-aware framework for modeling patient-specific disease trajectories from sparse, noisy, and irregular longitudinal data. The framework combines three methodological components: (1) latent state-space models for individualized temporal dynamics, (2) multimodal fusion for clinical, biomarker, and imaging features, and (3) uncertainty-aware validation and adaptive updating for robust digital twin operation. We also outline how conditional generative models can support data augmentation and stress testing for underrepresented progression patterns. As a preliminary feasibility study, we analyze longitudinal TADPOLE trajectories and show clear separation between cognitively normal and Alzheimer's disease cohorts in ADAS13, ventricle volume, and hippocampal volume over five years. We further conduct a multimodal next-visit prediction ablation using an LSTM sequence model on 3{,}003 visit-pair sequences derived from TADPOLE, where the combined cognitive plus MRI configuration achieves the lowest standardized RMSE for both ADAS13 (0.4419) and ventricle volume (0.5842), outperforming a Last Observation Carried Forward baseline. A Bayesian tensor modeling component for high-dimensional imaging fusion is also discussed. These results support the feasibility of the proposed architecture while also highlighting the need for stronger uncertainty calibration and longer-horizon predictive evaluation. The PCD-DT framework provides a principled starting point for personalized in silico modeling in neurodegenerative disease. This work positions PCD-DT as a foundational step toward clinically deployable, uncertainty-aware digital twin systems.

</details>


### [128] [When Roles Fail: Epistemic Constraints on Advocate Role Fidelity in LLM-Based Political Statement Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27228)
*Juergen Dietrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统实证检验了多智能体LLM管道中角色保真度的假设，发现模型普遍无法稳定维持分配的角色，识别出两种失败模式（认知地板效应和角色-先验冲突）并归因为统一的认知角色覆盖机制，且模型选择显著影响角色保真度表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有民主话语分析系统依赖多智能体LLM管道，假设评估模型能可靠维持分配的对立角色以生成多视角评估，但该核心假设从未被系统检验过。

Method: 开发了不依赖表面词汇的认知立场分类器，使用TRUST管道测量60条政治陈述（30英、30德）上的角色保真度，采用RDI、EDD、DDI、ERS四个指标。

Result: Mistral Large角色保真度67%，优于Claude Sonnet的39%；Claude表现出主动切换至对立立场的失败模式，而Mistral表现为角色放弃但不反转极性；事实核查提供者（Perplexity）对Claude的德语陈述保真度有显著负面影响（Delta=-15pp, p=0.007），但对Mistral无影响。

Conclusion: 未经角色保真度测量的多智能体LLM系统可能系统性地歪曲其所设计提供的认知多样性，对系统验证具有直接实践意义。

Abstract: Democratic discourse analysis systems increasingly rely on multi-agent LLM pipelines in which distinct evaluator models are assigned adversarial roles to generate structured, multi-perspective assessments of political statements. A core assumption is that models will reliably maintain their assigned roles. This paper provides the first systematic empirical test of that assumption using the TRUST pipeline. We develop an epistemic stance classifier that identifies advocate roles from reasoning text without relying on surface vocabulary, and measure role fidelity across 60 political statements (30 English, 30 German) using four metrics: Role Drift Index (RDI), Expected Drift Distance (EDD), Directional Drift Index (DDI), and Entropy-based Role Stability (ERS). We identify two failure modes - the Epistemic Floor Effect (fact-check results create an absolute lower bound below which the legitimizing role cannot be maintained) and Role-Prior Conflict (training-time knowledge overrides role instructions for factually unambiguous statements) - as manifestations of a single mechanism: Epistemic Role Override (ERO). Model choice significantly affects role fidelity: Mistral Large outperforms Claude Sonnet by 28pp (67% vs. 39%) and exhibits a qualitatively different failure mode - role abandonment without polarity reversal - compared to Claude's active switch to the opposing stance. Role fidelity is language-robust. Fact-check provider choice is not universally neutral: Perplexity significantly reduces Claude's role fidelity on German statements (Delta = -15pp, p = 0.007) while leaving Mistral unaffected. These findings have direct implications for multi-agent LLM validation: a system validated without role fidelity measurement may systematically misrepresent the epistemic diversity it was designed to provide.

</details>


### [129] [OptimusKG: Unifying biomedical knowledge in a modern multimodal graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27269)
*Lucas Vittor,Ayush Noori,Iñaki Arango,Joaquín Polonuer,Sam Rodriques,Andrew White,David A. Clifton,Marinka Zitnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OptimusKG是一个多模态生物医学带标签属性图（LPG），整合了18个本体和受控词表中的结构化与半结构化数据，包含190,531个节点、21,813,816条边和67,249,863个属性实例，覆盖分子、解剖、临床和环境领域。通过PaperQA3验证，70.0%的采样边获得文献支持，83.4%的假边未获支持，表明该图能捕捉尚未在科学文献中综合的生物医学知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生物医学知识图谱要么从非结构化文档中派生而缺乏模式级约束，要么从结构化资源组装但难以统一表示。需要一种既能保留结构化资源中事实性、类型特定元数据，又能统一整合多源异构数据的知识图谱。

Method: 从18个本体和受控词表中提取结构化与半结构化数据，构建多模态带标签属性图（LPG）；强制执行节点和边的顶层模式，同时保留细粒度、类型特定的属性、交叉引用和溯源信息；使用PaperQA3多模态智能体评估图中边是否得到科学文献的证据支持。

Result: OptimusKG包含190,531个节点（10种实体类型）、21,813,816条边（26种关系类型）、67,249,863个属性实例（110,276,843个值，150个属性键）。PaperQA3验证显示70.0%的采样边有文献支持，83.4%的假边未获支持。未获文献支持的边主要来自实验和功能基因组学资源。

Conclusion: OptimusKG能够捕捉可能先于科学文献综合的生物医学知识，并以Apache Parquet文件形式分发，为基于图的机器学习、知识增强的大语言模型检索以及假设生成等生物医学发现用例提供标准化资源。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) are widely used in the life sciences, yet many are derived from unstructured documents and therefore lack schema-level constrains, whereas graphs assembled from structured resources are difficult to harmonize into a unified representation. We present OptimusKG, a multimodal biomedical labeled property graph (LPG) built from structured and semi-structured resources to preserve factual, type-specific metadata across molecular, anatomical, clinical, and environmental domains. OptimusKG contains 190,531 nodes across 10 entity types, 21,813,816 edges across 26 relation types, and 67,249,863 property instances encoding 110,276,843 values across 150 distinct property keys, derived from 18 ontologies and controlled vocabularies. The graph enforces a top-level schema for nodes and edges and retains granular, type-specific properties, cross-references, and provenance across molecular, anatomical, clinical, and environmental domains. We assessed the validity of OptimusKG by evaluating whether graph relationships are supported by evidence from the scientific literature using a multimodal agent, PaperQA3. PaperQA3 identified supporting evidence for 70.0% of sampled edges, whereas 83.4% of sampled false edges received no supporting evidence. Edges without literature support were concentrated in associations derived from experimental and functional genomics resources, suggesting that OptimusKG captures biomedical knowledge that may precede synthesis in the scientific literature. OptimusKG is distributed as Apache Parquet files, providing a standardized resource for graph-based machine learning, knowledge-grounded retrieval with large language models, and biomedical discovery use cases such as hypothesis generation.

</details>


### [130] [The Inverse-Wisdom Law: Architectural Tribalism and the Consensus Paradox in Agentic Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27274)
*Dahlia Shehata,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示多智能体系统中的“共识悖论”：当智能体群体优先追求内部架构一致性而非外部逻辑真值时，增加逻辑智能体反而会固化错误轨迹。通过36个实验、12,804条轨迹验证了“逆智慧定律”，并提出了部落系数、谄媚权重等机制性指标，最终建立“异质性指令”作为弹性架构的安全要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战多智能体系统研究中“群体智慧”这一隐含假设，揭示智能体协作中内部共识与外部真理之间的根本性矛盾。

Method: 在GAIA、Multi-Challenge、SWE-bench三个SOTA基准上，使用Gemini 3.1 Pro、Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5.4三种前沿模型，进行36组实验（含12,804条轨迹），分析亲缘主导型群体中逻辑智能体数量与轨迹错误稳定性之间的关系。

Result: 证明了逆智慧定律：在亲缘主导的群体中，增加逻辑智能体反而增加错误轨迹的稳定性；存在逻辑饱和点，此时内部熵为零而事实错误率为1；终端群体完整性严格由合成器的接收逻辑门控，而非聚合智能体质量。

Conclusion: 定义了部落系数和谄媚权重作为智能体群体失效的主要机制性决定因素，并提出了异质性指令作为弹性多智能体架构的基础安全要求。

Abstract: As AI transitions toward multi-agent systems (MAS) to solve complex workflows, research paradigms operate on the axiomatic assumption that agent collaboration mirrors the "Wisdom of the Crowd". We challenge this assumption by formalizing the Consensus Paradox: a phenomenon where agentic swarms prioritize internal architectural agreement over external logical truth. Through a 36 experiments encompassing 12,804 trajectories across three state-of-the-art (SOTA) benchmarks (GAIA, Multi-Challenge, and SWE-bench), we prove the Inverse-Wisdom Law: in kinship-dominant swarms, adding logical agents increases the stability of erroneous trajectories rather than the probability of truth. The introduction of additional logical audits converges the system toward a Logic Saturation where internal entropy hits zero while factual error hits unity. By evaluating the interaction between the 3 preeminent SOTA models (Gemini 3.1 Pro, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and GPT-5.4), we establish the Architectural Tribalism Asymmetry as a mechanistic law of transformer weights. We demonstrate that terminal swarm integrity is strictly gated by the synthesizer's receptive logic, rather than aggregate agent quality. We define the Tribalism Coefficient and the Sycophantic Weight as the primary mechanistic determinants of swarm failure. Finally, we establish the Heterogeneity Mandate as a foundational safety requirement for resilient agentic architectures.

</details>


### [131] [Mechanized Foundations of Structural Governance: Machine-Checked Proofs for Governed Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27289)
*Alan L. McCann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在认知工作流系统的结构治理理论上提出五项成果（三项Coq机械化证明，两项纸上归约证明），以及第六项贡献：将抽象模型与BEAM运行时环境通过形式化规范与属性测试（70,000+随机序列，零分歧）相连接。机械化实现约12,000行，36个模块，454个定理，零未证明引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为认知工作流系统建立形式化的结构治理理论，确保无限程序行为的安全性，并统一跨元递归塔的治理语义，同时证明表达完备性与必要组件的数学必然性。

Method: 使用Coq 8.19与Interaction Trees库的参量化余归纳实现三项机械化证明（治理安全谓词、治理不变性定理、充分性定理）；通过显式归约完成纸上证明（交替范式、必要性定理）；使用属性测试（70,000+随机生成指令序列）验证BEAM运行时规范一致性。

Result: 建立了索引布尔权限标志的余归纳安全谓词（未经治理I/O为假，治理解释为真）；证明了治理在元递归塔上的定义等价性；证明了四个原子原语（代码、推理、记忆、调用）在Kleisli范畴合成闭包下的表达完备性；给出了任意机器的交替代码-效应层规范分解及合流重写系统；通过Rice定理归约证明了架构不透明组件的数学必要性。

Conclusion: 形式化治理理论为认知工作流系统提供了严密的理论基础，并通过运行时验证证明了理论模型与实际系统的一致性。

Abstract: We present five results in the theory of structural governance for cognitive workflow systems. Three are mechanized in Coq 8.19 using the Interaction Trees library with parameterized coinduction; two are proved on paper with explicit reductions. The Coinductive Safety Predicate (gov_safe) is a coinductive property that captures governance safety for infinite program behaviors, indexed by a boolean permission flag that is provably false for ungoverned I/O and true for governed interpretations (mechanized). The Governance Invariance Theorem establishes that governance is uniform across the meta-recursive tower: governance at level n+1 reduces to governance at level n by definitional equality of the type (mechanized). The Sufficiency Theorem proves that four atomic primitives (code, reason, memory, call) are expressively complete for any discrete intelligent system, formalized as compositional closure of a Kleisli category (mechanized). The Alternating Normal Form provides a canonical decomposition of any machine into alternating code and effect layers, with a confluent rewriting system (paper proof). The Necessity Theorem proves via explicit reduction to Rice's theorem that an architecturally opaque component (the reason primitive) is mathematically necessary for problems requiring semantic judgment (paper proof). A sixth contribution connects the abstract model to the deployed runtime: the Verified Interpreter Specification formalizes the BEAM runtime's trust, capability, and hash chain logic in Coq, then tests the running system against this specification using property-based testing with over 70,000 randomly generated directive sequences and zero disagreements. The mechanization comprises approximately 12,000 lines across 36 modules with 454 theorems and zero admitted lemmas.

</details>


### [132] [The Two Boundaries: Why Behavioral AI Governance Fails Structurally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27292)
*Alan L. McCann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"共域治理"概念：AI系统的表达能力边界与治理边界必须一致，否则必然产生未治理风险或无效治理。基于Rice定理证明，对于图灵完备架构，行为层面的效果治理在一般情况下不可判定，因此必须在架构层面将计算与效果分离，使治理检查嵌入执行管线而非事后附加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统中，表达能力和治理范围独立定义，导致三种区域中两种是失效模式：未治理的能力（风险）和针对不存在能力的治理（作秀）。需要一种正式框架来分析这一结构性缺口。

Method: 形式化定义表达能力边界与治理边界；引用Rice定理（1953）证明行为治理在一般情况下不可判定；提出"共域治理"作为系统属性，要求架构层面分离计算与效果；在Coq中机械化验证了454个定理。

Result: 共域治理需要架构决策（分离计算与效果）而非事后增加治理层；结构治理下治理检查成为执行管线的一部分；给出了可测试的判据：要么两边界可证明一致，否则风险与作秀在结构上不可避免。

Conclusion: 共域治理是AI治理系统的可测试标准。论文区分了"效果治理"（API调用、数据库写入等实际行为）与"模型输出治理"（内容质量、偏见等），认为两者需要不同机制。

Abstract: Every system that performs effects has two boundaries: what it can do (expressiveness) and what governance covers (governance). In nearly all deployed AI systems, these boundaries are defined independently, creating three regions: governed capabilities (the only useful region), ungoverned capabilities (risk), and governance policies that address non-existent capabilities (theater). Two of the three regions are failure modes. We focus on the governance of effects: actions that AI systems perform in the world (API calls, database writes, tool invocations). This is distinct from the governance of model outputs (content quality, bias, fairness), which operates at a different level and requires different mechanisms. We present a formal framework for analyzing this structural gap. Rice's theorem (1953) proves the gap is undecidable in the general case for any Turing-complete architecture that attempts to govern effects behaviorally: no algorithm can decide non-trivial semantic properties of arbitrary programs, including the property "this program's effects comply with the governance policy." We define coterminous governance: a system property where the expressivenessboundary equals the governance boundary. We show that coterminous governance requires an architectural decision (separatingcomputation from effect) rather than a governance layer added after the fact. We show that structural governance under this separation subsumes separate governance infrastructure: governance checks become part of the execution pipeline rather than a second system running alongside it. We propose coterminous governance as the testable criterion for any AI governance system: either the two boundaries are provably identical, or risk and theater are structurally inevitable. Proofs are mechanized in Coq (454 theorems, 36 modules, 0 admitted).

</details>


### [133] [Learning Rate Engineering: From Coarse Single Parameter to Layered Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27295)
*Ming-Hong Yao,Di Wang,Jian Cui,Jin-Yan Chen,Zi-Hao Cui,Fa Wang,Chen Wei,Qiu-Ye Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了学习率调度策略从全局固定学习率到分层时间联合调度的五代演进，提出判别式自适应层缩放（DALS）统一框架，并在多数据集上验证了其有效性，同时揭示了不同训练范式下策略的显著差异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移学习中存在"不可能三角"：低层需要小更新以保留通用知识，高层需要大更新以适应新任务。统一的学习率策略无法同时满足这两类需求，因此需要层级别与时间维度的联合调度。

Method: 提出DALS框架，融合了相位自适应余弦调度、深度感知的Grokfast梯度滤波和LARS风格信任比；将学习率调度演进归纳为五代：Gen1全局固定、Gen2全局调度、Gen3参数级自适应、Gen4层级差异化、Gen5层级-时间联合调度。

Result: 在合成数据上DALS达到98.0%最佳准确率，DALS-Fast仅3个epoch达到90%；STLR+Discriminative在从头训练任务上严重失败（TREC-6仅43.6%，RAdam为96.8%），证明方向性衰减偏置在无预训练特征时有害。

Conclusion: 没有单一策略在所有训练范式中占优；DALS避免了极端表现，在合成数据上最佳同时保持有竞争力的微调性能，验证了层级-时间联合调度的优势。

Abstract: Learning rate scheduling has evolved from the single global fixed rate of early SGD to sophisticated layer-wise adaptive strategies. We systematize this evolution into five generations: (Gen1) global fixed learning rates, (Gen2) global scheduling, (Gen3) parameter-level adaptation, (Gen4) layer-level differentiation, and (Gen5) joint layer-time scheduling. We trace the fundamental motivation behind each transition, showing how the shift from one-size-fits-all to tailoring by layer and time addresses the impossible trinity of transfer learning: lower layers require small updates to preserve general knowledge while higher layers need large updates to adapt to new tasks. Building on this taxonomy, we propose Discriminative Adaptive Layer Scaling (DALS), a unified framework that integrates phase-adaptive cosine scheduling, depth-aware Grokfast gradient filtering, and LARS-style trust ratios into a single coherent optimizer. We benchmark 18 strategies including three DALS variants across all five generations on five datasets: synthetic, CIFAR-10 (from scratch), RTE, TREC-6, and IMDb (fine-tuning). On synthetic, DALS achieves the best accuracy at 98.0%, while DALS-Fast reaches 90% in just 3 epochs. The cross-dataset analysis reveals striking regime-dependent patterns -- no single strategy wins across all regimes. Critically, STLR+Discriminative, the ULMFiT champion, catastrophically fails on from-scratch tasks (43.6% on TREC-6 from scratch vs. 96.8% with RAdam), confirming that directional decay biases are harmful without pretrained features. DALS avoids either extreme, achieving the best synthetic result while maintaining competitive fine-tuning performance.

</details>


### [134] [METASYMBO: Multi-Agent Language-Guided Metamaterial Discovery via Symbolic Latent Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27300)
*Jianpeng Chen,Wangzhi Zhan,Dongqi Fu,Junkai Zhang,Zian Jia,Ling Li,Wei Wang,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MetaSymbO 是一个多智能体框架，通过符号驱动的潜在进化实现语言引导的超材料发现，无需显式数值目标即可解释自然语言设计意图，在结构有效性、语言引导得分和新颖性方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超材料逆向设计方法需要明确的数值属性目标，不适用于早期探索阶段研究者以自然语言表达的模糊约束和定性意图。大语言模型能理解意图但缺乏几何感知和物理属性有效性验证。

Method: 提出 MetaSymbO 多智能体框架，包含三个智能体：Designer（解释自由形式设计意图并检索语义一致的骨架）、Generator（在解耦潜在空间中合成候选微结构）、Supervisor（提供快速属性感知反馈进行迭代优化）。引入符号驱动潜在进化，通过可编程算子对解耦潜在因子进行组合、修改和微调。

Result: 相比现有基线，对称性提升34%，周期性提升近98%；语言引导得分比先进推理LLM高约6-7%；符号逻辑算子在可编程语义对齐上有效；在拉胀、高刚度超材料设计中验证了实际能力。

Conclusion: MetaSymbO 成功弥合了自然语言设计意图与几何结构生成之间的鸿沟，为早期超材料探索提供了高效、灵活的新范式。

Abstract: Metamaterial discovery seeks microstructured materials whose geometry induces targeted mechanical behavior. Existing inverse-design methods can efficiently generate candidates, but they typically require explicit numerical property targets and are less suitable for early-stage exploration, where researchers often begin with incomplete constraints and qualitative intents expressed in natural language. Large language models can interpret such intents, but they lack geometric awareness and physical property validity. To address this gap, we propose MetaSymbO, a multi-agent framework for language-guided Metamaterial discovery via Symbolic-driven latent evOlution. Specifically, MetaSymbO contains three agents: a Designer that interprets free-form design intents and retrieves a semantically consistent scaffold, a Generator that synthesizes candidate microstructures in a disentangled latent space, and a Supervisor that provides fast property-aware feedback for iterative refinement. To move beyond the limitations of reproducing known samples from literature and training data, we further introduce symbolic-driven latent evolution, which applies programmable operators over disentangled latent factors to compose, modify, and refine structures at inference time. Extensive experiments demonstrate that (i) MetaSymbO improves structural validity by up to 34% in symmetry and nearly 98% in periodicity compared to state-of-the-art baselines; (ii) MetaSymbO achieves about 6-7% higher language-guidance scores while maintaining superior structure novelty compared to advanced reasoning LLMs; (iii) qualitative analyses confirm the effectiveness of symbolic logic operators in enabling programmable semantic alignment; and (iv) realworld case studies on auxetic, high-stiffness metamaterial design further validate its practical capability.

</details>


### [135] [Investigating More Explainable and Partition-Free Compositionality Estimation for LLMs: A Rule-Generation Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27340)
*Ziyao Xu,Cong Wang,Houfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于规则生成的新视角来评估LLM的组合性，通过让模型生成程序作为数据集映射规则，并基于复杂度理论提供估计，从而克服了传统组合泛化测试仅关注输出结果、存在可解释性缺陷和组合泄露的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统组合泛化测试存在两个主要局限：(1)仅关注输出结果，未考虑LLM对样本组合性的理解，缺乏可解释性；(2)依赖数据集划分形成测试集，存在组合泄露问题。因此需要一种新的组合性评估方法。

Method: 提出规则生成视角：要求LLM生成程序作为数据集映射的规则，并基于复杂度理论来估计LLM的组合性。在字符串到网格（string-to-grid）任务上对现有先进LLM进行实验和分析。

Result: 基于该视角在string-to-grid任务上对现有先进LLM进行实验，发现了LLM表现出的各种组合性特征和组合性缺陷。

Conclusion: 规则生成视角克服了传统组合泛化测试的局限性，为分析LLM的组合性表征提供了新途径。

Abstract: Compositional generalization tests are often used to estimate the compositionality of LLMs. However, such tests have the following limitations: (1) they only focus on the output results without considering LLMs' understanding of sample compositionality, resulting in explainability defects; (2) they rely on dataset partition to form the test set with combinations unseen in the training set, suffering from combination leakage issues. In this work, we propose a novel rule-generation perspective for compositionality estimation for LLMs. It requires LLMs to generate a program as rules for dataset mapping and provides estimates of the compositionality of LLMs using complexity-based theory. The perspective addresses the limitations of compositional generalization tests and provides a new way to analyze the compositionality characterization of LLMs. We conduct experiments and analysis of existing advanced LLMs based on this perspective on a string-to-grid task, and find various compositionality characterizations and compositionality deficiencies exhibited by LLMs.

</details>


### [136] [Heterogeneous Scientific Foundation Model Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27351)
*Zihao Li,Jiaru Zou,Feihao Fang,Xuying Ning,Mengting Ai,Tianxin Wei,Sirui Chen,Xiyuan Yang,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Eywa是一个异构智能体框架，通过将领域特定基础模型与语言模型推理接口相结合，扩展了基于语言的智能体系统，使其能够处理科学领域的非语言数据模态，在物理、生命和社会科学任务中取得了改进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型智能体系统过度依赖语言作为通用接口，这限制了其在科学领域的应用——因为许多科学任务需要处理非语言数据，而针对这些领域已开发了专门的基础模型，但这些模型无法直接融入以语言为中心的智能体系统中。

Method: Eywa通过为领域特定基础模型配备基于语言模型的推理接口，让语言模型能够引导对非语言数据模态的推理。框架包含三种集成模式：单智能体管线(EywaAgent)、多智能体系统(EywaMAS)和基于规划的编排框架(EywaOrchestra)，后者通过规划器动态协调传统智能体和Eywa智能体处理异构数据模态的复杂任务。

Result: 在物理、生命和社会科学等多个科学领域的实验表明，Eywa在涉及结构化和领域特定数据的任务上提升了性能，同时通过与专业基础模型的有效协作，减少了对基于语言推理的依赖。

Conclusion: Eywa证明了将语言模型推理能力与领域特定基础模型相结合的有效性，为科学领域中处理异构数据模态的智能体系统提供了一种可行的架构设计思路。

Abstract: Agentic large language model systems have demonstrated strong capabilities. However, their reliance on language as the universal interface fundamentally limits their applicability to many real-world problems, especially in scientific domains where domain-specific foundation models have been developed to address specialized tasks beyond natural language. In this work, we introduce Eywa, a heterogeneous agentic framework designed to extend language-centric systems to a broader class of scientific foundation models. The key idea of Eywa is to augment domain-specific foundation models with a language-model-based reasoning interface, enabling language models to guide inference over non-linguistic data modalities. This design allows predictive foundation models, which are typically optimized for specialized data and tasks, to participate in higher-level reasoning and decision-making processes within agentic systems. Eywa can serve as a drop-in replacement for a single-agent pipeline (EywaAgent) or be integrated into existing multi-agent systems by replacing traditional agents with specialized agents (EywaMAS). We further investigate a planning-based orchestration framework in which a planner dynamically coordinates traditional agents and Eywa agents to solve complex tasks across heterogeneous data modalities (EywaOrchestra). We evaluate Eywa across a diverse set of scientific domains spanning physical, life, and social sciences. Experimental results demonstrate that Eywa improves performance on tasks involving structured and domain-specific data, while reducing reliance on language-based reasoning through effective collaboration with specialized foundation models.

</details>


### [137] [Measurement Risk in Supervised Financial NLP: Rubric and Metric Sensitivity on JF-ICR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27374)
*Sidi Chang,Peiying Zhu,Yuxiao Chen,Rongdong Chai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了金融NLP基准测试中标注可靠性、度量选择和排名稳定性问题，发现标注词措变化会导致标签显著变化（R2-R3一致性70.0%-83.4%），并非所有度量在类别不平衡下都有效（精确准确率、宏F1和加权κ是可识别度量），且排名稳定性仅在对度量进行可识别性审计后才得以提升。贡献在于提出了一种面向监督式金融基准的评估治理规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当LLM被用于读取财报电话会议等金融文本时，监督式金融NLP基准被用作模型选择的决策依据。但基准评分本身受标注词措、度量选择和聚合策略的影响存在"测量风险"，这种风险挑战了标注客观性的隐含假设。

Method: 在日本金融隐含承诺识别（JF-ICR）基准上设计多因素交叉实验，包括：253条测试样本×4个前沿LLM×5种标注词措×3种温度×5个序数度量。通过分析标注一致性、度量可识别性和排名聚合稳定性来量化测量风险。

Result: 标注词措变化导致R2-R3一致性仅70.0%-83.4%，+1/0边界处变动最大；精确准确率、宏F1和加权κ是唯一可识别的度量，而"within-one准确率"过于宽松，"最差类准确率"噪声过大；在可识别度量子集上，Bradley-Terry、Borda和Ranked Pairs排名一致，而在全量五度量上最近相邻对出现分歧。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献并非提出新的排行榜，而是为存在标注且需评估治理的监督式金融基准建立了一套报告规范，强调在基准发布前必须进行度量可识别性审计以增强排名主张的可辩护性。

Abstract: As LLMs become credible readers of earnings calls, investor-relations Q\&A, guidance, and disclosure language, supervised financial NLP benchmarks increasingly function as decision evidence for model selection and deployment. A hidden assumption is that gold labels make such evidence objective. This assumption breaks down when the benchmark ruler itself is sensitive to rubric wording, metric choice, or aggregation policy. We study this measurement risk on Japanese Financial Implicit-Commitment Recognition (JF-ICR; a pinned 253-item test split x 4 frontier LLMs x 5 rubrics x 3 temperatures x 5 ordinal metrics). Three findings follow. First, rubric wording materially changes model-assigned labels: R2--R3 agreement ranges from 70.0% to 83.4%, with the dominant movement near the +1 / 0 implicit-commitment boundary. This pattern is consistent with a pragmatic-boundary interpretation, but is not a validated linguistic-causality claim because the present rubric variants confound semantics, examples, and verbosity. Second, not every metric remains informative under the JF-ICR class distribution. Within-one accuracy is too easy because near misses receive credit and the majority class dominates; worst-class accuracy is too noisy because the rarest class has only two examples. Exact accuracy, macro-F1, and weighted \k{appa} are therefore the identifiable metrics under our operational rule. Third, ranking claims become more defensible only after this metric-identifiability audit: Bradley--Terry, Borda, and Ranked Pairs agree on the identifiable metric subset, while the full five-metric sweep produces disagreement on the closest pair. The contribution is not a new leaderboard, but a reporting discipline for supervised financial benchmarks whose gold labels exist and whose evaluation ruler still requires governance.

</details>


### [138] [Robust Learning on Heterogeneous Graphs with Heterophily: A Graph Structure Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27387)
*Yihan Zhang,Ercan E. Kuruoglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HGUL框架，通过kNN图构建、图结构学习和异构亲和学习三个模块，联合处理异质图中的异质性（heterophily）和结构噪声问题，在多种数据集上表现出优越的鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质图在建模复杂系统中广泛使用，但现有方法在处理异质性（heterophily）和结构噪声时存在不足，尤其当图中存在误导性连接时模型性能显著下降。本文旨在解决这一未被充分探索的问题。

Method: 提出HGUL统一框架，包含三个互补模块：(1) kNN图构建模块，恢复可靠的局部邻域；(2) 图结构学习模块，通过过滤噪声边自适应优化邻接矩阵；(3) 异构亲和学习模块，利用多项式图核扩展的亲和矩阵捕获类级关系。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，HGUL在干净图上持续优于现有方法，并在不同结构噪声水平下保持强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 联合建模异质性（heterophily）和噪声对于异质图学习至关重要，HGUL框架为此提供了一种有效的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous graphs with heterophily have emerged as a powerful abstraction for modeling complex real-world systems, where nodes of different types and labels interact in diverse and often non-homophilous ways. Despite recent advances, robust representation learning for such graphs remains largely unexplored, particularly in the presence of noisy or misleading connectivity. In this work, we investigate this problem and identify structural noise as a critical challenge that significantly degrades model performance. To address this issue, we propose a unified framework, Heterogeneous Graph Unified Learning (HGUL), which jointly handles heterophily and noisy graph structures. The framework consists of three complementary modules: a kNN-based graph construction module that recovers reliable local neighborhoods, a graph structure learning module that adaptively refines the adjacency by filtering noisy edges, and a heterogeneous affinity learning module that captures class-level relationships via an extended affinity matrix derived from a polynomial graph kernel. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that HGUL consistently outperforms existing methods on clean graphs and maintains strong robustness under varying levels of structural noise. The results further underscore the importance of jointly modeling heterophily and noise in heterogeneous graph learning.

</details>


### [139] [Leading Across the Spectrum of Human-AI Relationships: A Conceptual Framework for Increasingly Heterogeneous Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27392)
*Alejandro R. Jadad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个领导力视角下的决策光谱（纯人类、半人马、平等、米诺陶、纯AI），用于识别人类与AI协作决策中领导权实际所在，核心风险是"误认"——领导者可能误判谁在真正主导决策过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作决策中的权力分配日益模糊：决策可能看似由人类主导，实则AI已设定了框架；或看似自动化，实则人类判断仍具决定性力量。领导者需要一种工具来看清这些关系。

Method: 提出了五位置光谱（Pure Human, Centaur, Co-equal, Minotaur, Pure AI），以"领导工作发生在哪里"为核心判断标准：谁定义问题框架、谁重定向工作、谁对结果负责。引入"共适应性"概念，置于异构团队协作的语境中。

Result: 该框架帮助领导者识别决策配置（包括层叠、漂移和变化），警示三种误认风险：保持人本叙事而决策权已转移、监督变成仪式性、人类在场反而使决策更糟。

Conclusion: 这些配置将塑造组织中的权力、责任和信任分配。未来是否可治理和值得居住，取决于领导者能否及早看清实际决策是如何被塑造的。

Abstract: What shapes a consequential decision when human and artificial intelligence work on it together? The answer is becoming harder to see. A decision may look human-led after AI has set the frame, or appear automated while human judgment still carries decisive force. This paper offers a leadership-facing spectrum to see those relationships within a bounded mandate: Pure Human, Centaur (human-dominant, with AI in the loop), Co-equal, Minotaur (AI-dominant, with humans in the loop), and Pure AI.
  The spectrum asks where leadership work occurs: who frames the problem, who redirects the work, and who can answer for what follows. The five positions are landmarks that help leaders recognize configurations as they layer, drift, or change in a single decision. The central risk is misrecognition: leaders may keep a human-centered story in place after decision-shaping authority has shifted elsewhere. They may believe oversight remains meaningful when it has become ceremonial, or keep humans in the loop when their involvement could make the decision worse. The framework introduces co-adaptability, the capacity of a configuration to improve as human and non-human participants adjust together, and places it within heterogeneous teaming, where participants may vary by number, substrate, model architecture, capability, speed, memory, and form of participation.
  The aim is practical: to help strategic leaders and those designing or deploying AI systems recognize the configuration at work, notice when it shifts, and judge whether it fits the decision before them. These configurations will shape how power, responsibility, and trust are distributed in organizational life. Whether the futures they help create remain governable and worth inhabiting will depend on leaders who can see, early enough, where and how consequential decisions are actually being shaped.

</details>


### [140] [InteractWeb-Bench: Can Multimodal Agent Escape Blind Execution in Interactive Website Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27419)
*Qiyao Wang,Haoran Hu,Longze Chen,Hongbo Wang,Hamid Alinejad-Rokny,Yuan Lin,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了InteractWeb-Bench，这是首个在非专业低代码用户条件下评估多模态大模型网站生成能力的交互式基准，揭示了前沿模型在模糊指令下存在"盲目执行"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准假设输入信息结构化且完整、执行环境静态，但真实开发场景中非专业用户的指令往往模糊、低质量，导致模型与用户意图之间存在语义错配，即"盲目执行"问题，现有基准无法评估这一关键瓶颈。

Method: 构建InteractWeb-Bench基准，包含四种用户智能体和基于需求工程缺陷分类法的人设驱动指令扰动（歧义、冗余、矛盾），并开发交互式执行环境，定义统一动作空间（澄清、实现、验证、提交），支持迭代意图精炼、代码合成和可视化反馈验证。

Result: 实验表明，前沿MLLM基智能体仍陷入盲目执行模式，在意图识别和自适应交互方面存在明显局限。

Conclusion: 现有MLLM在非专业用户的低质量指令下无法有效识别真实意图并进行交互修正，需要向更具交互性和自适应能力的智能体方向发展。

Abstract: With the advancement of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and coding agents, the website development has shifted from manual programming to agent-based project-level code synthesis. Existing benchmarks rely on idealized assumptions, especially for well-structured, information-rich inputs and static execution settings. In contrast, real-world development is constrained by a critical bottleneck: the semantic misalignment between ambiguous, low-quality instructions from non-expert users and model understanding, which results in a failure mode that we term blind execution. To address this gap, we introduce InteractWeb-Bench, the first multimodal interactive benchmark for website generation under non-expert low-code user conditions. InteractWeb-Bench introduces four types of user agents and persona-driven instruction perturbations to systematically simulate diverse user behaviors, including ambiguity, redundancy, and contradiction, grounded in requirement engineering defect taxonomies. We develop an interactive execution environment for agents, featuring a unified action space comprising Clarify, Implement, Verify, and Submit, enabling iterative intent refinement, code synthesis, and visual feedback-based validation. Extensive experiments and analysis reveal that frontier MLLM-based agents remain trapped in blind execution, exposing limitations in intent recognition and adaptive interaction.

</details>


### [141] [Belief-Guided Inference Control for Large Language Model Services via Verifiable Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27536)
*Wenhao Yuan,Chenchen Lin,Jian Chen,Jinfeng Xu,Shuo Yang,Edith Cheuk Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Veroic框架，将黑盒LLM推理控制建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），通过构建轻量级可验证观测通道聚合异构质量信号为信念状态，实现预算感知的自适应推理控制，在质量-成本权衡、风险估计和长期推理控制方面优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒LLM服务中，响应可靠性在决策时仅部分可观测，而更强的推理路径会带来显著计算成本，因此需要在默认低成本和高质量推理之间进行预算约束下的序贯决策。

Method: 提出Veroic框架，将请求级推理控制建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）；从输入-输出对构建轻量级可验证观测通道，通过聚合异构质量信号形成关于潜在响应可靠性的信念状态；由预算感知策略决定返回默认输出还是触发更高成本的推理路径。

Result: 在多种任务上，Veroic在质量-成本权衡、风险估计与校准、以及长期推理控制方面均优于竞争基线方法。

Conclusion: Veroic框架有效解决了黑盒LLM服务中预算约束下的自适应推理控制问题，实现了更优的质量-成本平衡和更稳健的长期推理控制。
